# iReport ports: deviation register

Every place an iReport port (`iReport_Ports/<Folder>/*.htm`) behaves differently
from the stock Fishbowl Jasper report it replaces, for review.

- **Review.** Tick the Reviewed column (initials + date) once a row is checked in the
  Fishbowl client. New ports add their rows here as they are built.
- **Sources.** Each port's header comment, read in full on 2026-10-14. Where a
  row comes from somewhere else it says so: "(README)" is
  `iReport_Ports/README.md`, "(code)" is a comment in the port's script.
- **Type.** *fix*: the stock report is wrong, and the Stock column says why.
  *addition*: a new feature. *omission*: a stock feature left out, and the
  Port column says why. *layout*: presentation only. *performance*: same
  result, faster. *data*: a different source with the same intent.
- **Evidence.** The figures the header gives; "not stated" when it has none.
  "Live" is the live Fishbowl database the ports were checked against.
- **Status.** Every port was desk-tested against the live database and in
  Chrome. **None has been run inside the Fishbowl client yet** (README), so
  every check below still needs repeating in the client.
- **Not faithful ports.** InventoryAvailability, AvailableToBuild,
  PartActivity and the three summaries (PurchaseOrderSummary,
  receivingsummary, SalesOrderSummary, the first BI versions of stock
  reports) are older BI reports moved into the tree. They list only what
  their header says about the PDF view and how they differ from the stock
  report.

## Common to every port

These apply to every port and are not repeated per report.

| Report | Area | Stock report does | Port does | Type | Evidence | How to verify | Reviewed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| All ports | Presentation | Static Jasper pages | REPORT chrome: push-down filters that apply on change, collapsible group rows carrying their totals, sticky totals footer | layout | not stated | Collapse a group: its row still carries the group totals |  |
| All ports | PDF view | Jasper PDF | App / PDF toggle (fb-doc): the stock paper and orientation, title band, repeated headings, groups "(continued)" over a break, "Page X of Y"; paper selector; font steps down to 6.5pt to fit | layout | Desk-tested in Chrome 2026-10-10; not tried in the client (README) | In the client: PDF view, then the toolbar's Save → PDF and Email; compare page count and paper with the stock PDF |  |
| All ports | Print | Prints the Jasper pages | Prints the PDF-view pages from either view; a plain printout without fb-doc | layout | not stated | Print from the App and PDF views: same pages |  |
| All ports | Large results | Every row | Main query capped at 10,000 rows, with a notice and Load all. The cut falls on a whole group, part, order or customer where a plain cut would corrupt a figure. Any filter change brings the cap back | performance | Per-report boundaries in the README table | Run a wide filter on a large DB: notice shows; Load all; totals of the groups shown don't change |  |
| All ports | Columns | Fixed | Resize, sort (asc / desc / report order) and drag to reorder, remembered per user; Settings → Reset column layout | addition | not stated | Move a column, reopen the report: layout kept; reset restores it |  |
| All ports | Extras | None | Record links (openModule), Excel export, a Pivot view where the README lists one | addition | not stated | Click a link; export to Excel and compare the totals with the screen |  |
| All ports | Number format | REPORT_DECIMAL_* patterns; currency symbol per field | The REPORT_DECIMAL_*_PRECISION properties and the home currency format | layout | not stated | Change a precision property: stock and port show the same decimals |  |

## Record documents (one record per run)

The ports that print one record: Sales Order Report, Sales Invoice, Purchase
Order Report, Pick Ticket, Packing Slip, Shipping Invoice, Receiving List, RMA
Report, Transfer Order Report, Work Order Traveler / Report / Instructions,
Manufacture Order Traveler / Projected Cost. Since 2026-10-16 they all choose
their record through fb-doc's FBDoc.Record; these rows apply to each.

| Report | Area | Stock report does | Port does | Type | Evidence | How to verify | Reviewed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| All record documents | Choosing the record | A prompt for the order number (soNum, poNum, woNum…) | A picker in the Filters panel: typeahead over the number (and customer / part where the document had it), status chips, previous / next, Refresh. Starts on the record report setting (adopted once per user), else the user's last, else the newest | addition | A/B against the earlier pages: same record, same queries (tools/report-harness/ab.js, 2026-10-16) | Open the document with no setting: it opens on your last record; type a number; step with the arrows |  |
| All record documents | Launched from a module | n/a | Opened for a record from a module's Apps menu (report_btn), that record shows and can't be changed: picker, previous / next and Refresh hidden; not remembered as the user's last | addition | ab.js launch scenario, every document | Open from the module's Apps menu: the picker is hidden |  |
| All record documents | Document output | n/a | Asked for its pages only (FB_LAUNCH output 'pdf', report_btn's Print (App) / Email (rich)), the document reports ready once laid out, or an error when there is no launch, the launch is another module's, or the record won't load. Before 2026-10-16 only the paperwork documents and the Work Order Traveler had the error case; the others reported ready for whatever record they opened | fix | ab.js output-wrong scenario: error on every document | Print (App) from a module whose record the document doesn't take: an error, not a page |  |
| Purchase Order Report, Pick Ticket, Receiving List, Transfer Order Report, Work Order Report / Instructions, Manufacture Order Traveler / Projected Cost | Report settings | n/a | A record report setting (Fishbowl's own picker: PO, PICK, RECEIPT, TO, WO, MO) and a Page_setup setting, as the paperwork documents have. The RMA Report has only Page_setup: Fishbowl has no RMA picker for BI reports | addition | Setting tests 2026-10-16: a substituted setting opens that record once; a later pick stands | BI Reports › the report › Parameters: set the record; open it |  |
| Manufacture Order Projected Cost | Page_setup | Letter landscape | The setting changes the paper size only; the orientation stays landscape (the columns are laid out for it) | layout | not stated | Set Page_setup to A4: A4 landscape |  |
| Manufacture Order Traveler | MO picker | n/a | The box lists the newest 20 MOs when opened empty (as the other documents); typed search, the * wildcard and Enter on an exact number unchanged | addition | not stated | Click into the empty box: a list appears |  |
| All record documents | Without fb-doc | n/a | The record can't be chosen without the fb-doc script (the earlier pages fell back to a plain number box) | omission | not stated | Deploy fb-doc first (scripts/README.md) |  |

| Report | Area | Stock report does | Port does | Type | Evidence | How to verify | Reviewed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Adjustment Report | Views | List only | List (the stock layout), By type (collapsible type rows with totals) and Pivot | addition | not stated | By type: the type totals add up to the Amount Total |  |
| COGS by Account Summary | Account filter | Free-text LIKE on the account name | Pick list of accounts (no wildcard pattern) | addition | not stated | Pick two accounts: same lines as two stock runs |  |
| COGS by Account Summary | Capped totals | n/a | When lines are capped, a GROUP BY query totals every account, so account rows and the Grand Total stay whole | performance | not stated | Over 10,000 lines: the Grand Total equals the stock's before Load all |  |
| Export Summary | SO Total Tax | The "newTax" variable adds once per group, from one line's values | Sum of the shown orders' tax | fix | not stated | SO with several taxable lines: section Total Tax = sum of the order Tax rows |  |
| Export Summary | Tax-line join | Joins the SO's tax lines (TAXPERCENT / FLATTAX), which no printed field reads; an SO with several tax lines duplicates its lines | Join dropped | fix | not stated | SO with two tax lines: line count = soitem count |  |
| Export Summary | Margin % | -100 % on a zero-price line | Blank | fix | not stated | $0 Sale line: Margin % blank |  |
| Export Summary | Order groups | Group key num + (datePosted == null): rows that aren't adjacent split an order into extra groups | Rows kept together by posted state: at most two groups per order | fix | not stated | Order with posted and unposted lines: two groups, totals = stock's combined |  |
| Export Summary | Ref. # row | Printed for every posting | Only when the posting has a reference | layout | not stated | Unexported posting: no empty Ref. # row |  |
| Export Summary | WO query | Built from WO joins, then date-filtered | Driven from postwo: the same row set, found faster (code) | performance | not stated | WO section for one month: rows and Total Adjustment = stock's |  |
| Export Summary | Number filters | SO / PO / WO number matched exactly | % or * accepted as a wildcard (code) | addition | not stated | SO number "10%": every matching SO |  |
| Export Summary | Sections | Three stacked subreports | One view per section on screen; PDF and Print still print all three | layout | not stated | PDF prints SO, PO, WO whatever the selector shows |  |
| Default Account Mapping | Row order | No ORDER BY: the database's order (the syskey index order on the live DB) | ORDER BY sysproperties.syskey | layout | Live (FB_OCT): stock query and port return the same 24 rows in the same order | Run both on one DB: same order |  |
| Default Account Mapping | Defaults with no account | INNER JOIN asaccount: a default with a blank sysvalue, or one pointing at a deleted account, is silently left out | Same by default. "Show defaults with no account" (off) also lists them as "Not set" / "No such account (id N)"; the query LEFT JOINs asaccount and flags the mapped rows | addition | Live: all 24 defaults mapped, so no difference on FB_OCT; synthetic rows (blank, id 99999) showed both labels | Clear one default account in Accounting settings: stock omits it; port lists it with the option on |  |
| Default Account Mapping | Account number and type | Account name only | "Show account number and type" (off) adds asaccount.accountNumber and asaccounttype.name (camel-case spaced); Excel always includes them | addition | Live: Accounts Payable → 800, Other Current Liability | Option on: values match the Chart of Accounts |  |
| Default Account Mapping | Filters | No parameters | "Account contains" over both columns, applied on screen without a new query | addition | "ship" → 3 rows (Shipping Accrual / Expense / Income) | Type "ship" |  |
| Default Account Mapping | Fishbowl Account labels | Server Java helper Util.addSpacesToCamelBackStr, "Non-Inventory" as is | JS copy of the helper (fbAccountName) | data | 0 differences on 20,000 random strings and on all 24 live keys (accountsPayable → Accounts Payable, cogsAccount → Cogs Account) | Compare the port's page with the stock PDF line by line |  |
| Default Account Mapping | Large results | (common row) main query capped at 10,000 rows | No RowCap: one row per default account | omission | 24 rows live; Fishbowl defines a fixed set | n/a |  |
| Default Account Mapping | Record links | (common row) openModule links | None: openModule has no chart-of-accounts module | omission | n/a | n/a |  |
| Default Account Mapping | PDF columns | Fixed 250pt / 52pt blank gap / 250pt | Columns fitted to their contents; Account still right-aligned at the right margin | layout | Letter portrait, 1 page, same as stock for 24 rows | PDF view next to the stock PDF |  |

Cross-check (README): the Adjustment Report's Scrap rows equal the Consumption
Report's totals (Back/Post Date, inactive parts, all location groups): 11,957
scraps, $2,938,056.05 against $2,938,064.64 (29 rows of rounding).

| Dormant Customers | Sorting | Sort by option | Header click sets Sort by / Order; while capped a sort change re-runs the query | addition | not stated | Capped run, sort by Total Spent desc: the top customers are the true top |  |

| Auto PO | Data source | Rows from the server helper `JRAutoPOHelper` → `LogicInventory.runAutoPo(ShowVendorPartNumber, vendorID, !OnlyShowDefaultPartsForVendor, IncludeNoROP/OUL, null, IncludeWhenShort…, qbClassID, locationGroupID, dateRange1, dateRange2, false, false)`; its SQL only fetches the company name. | Rows from the client bridge `getAutoPo(vendor, lg, qbClass, showVPN, includeNoRopOul, alwaysManufacture, start, end)` → `ReportHelperModule.getAutoPo` → the same `runAutoPo`, identical arguments except the 3rd (fixed `true` = every vendor's part). | data | Disassembled read-only from the 26.9.1 jars: reporthelpers `JRAutoPOHelper.<init>`, fbswing `BiBrowser.lambda$addGetAutoPoJavaScript$13`, fbcore `ReportHelperModule.getAutoPo`, `LogicInventory.runAutoPo`, fbrepository `PartRepository.loadAutoPoSearchDetails`. 6/6 stock runs replayed equal. | `node run.js apo_ird.html x.png steps_apo_cmp.js` → `RESULT 6 pass, 0 fail`; in Fishbowl: Settings → Show debug console, Run: the console logs each `getAutoPo(…)` call. |  |
| Auto PO | Field mapping | The helper's `Needed` field is the engine's suggested qty and its `Suggested` field is the reorder point (array slots 9 and 16), so the printed Needed / Reorder Pnt columns are right despite the names. | Needed = `qty_suggested`, Reorder Pnt = `reorder_level` (same values). | data | `AutoPoData.getObjectData()` slot order vs `JRAutoPOHelper.getFieldValue`; FOOD124 Needed 167 / Reorder Pnt 72.78289 in both. | Company wide, defaults: FOOD124 row. |  |
| Auto PO | OnlyShowDefaultPartsForVendor | The engine joins only the default vendor part: each part under its default vendor, or under no vendor when it has none. | The bridge can't pass it, so the page keeps the engine's row when it is the default vendor part (same vendor, same UOM); otherwise it keeps the engine's figures and re-works the purchase qty for the default vendor part with the engine's rules (Allocated − On Hand − On Order + Order Up To; zero left out; vendor minimum when `useMinQtyAutoGenPOs`; direct `uomconversion` row else 1:1; round up for count UOMs), or in the part's UOM with no vendor. With a vendor picked: only parts whose default vendor it is. Re-worked Needed is italic (tooltip says why). | data | LG BP + default only: FOOD187 stock **4,383 20kg** under Apex Coconuts, port 4,383 20kg (the engine's own row is the non-default Drum vendor part: 439 Drum). Company wide + default only: CONS050 stock under a blank heading "48", port under No Vendor 48 ea. Vendor Azapak + default only: stock nothing (its only vendor part isn't default), port nothing ("…none has this vendor as its default vendor"). | Filters → LG BP, Only show the default vendor's parts, Run → FOOD187 = 4,383 20kg (italic). |  |
| Auto PO | Minimum qty in default-only re-work | Engine: the vendor's FIRST vendor part (`getVendorPartList(part).filter(vendor).findFirst()`) supplies the minimum. | The vendor's vendor part with the lowest id. | data | Order of `getVendorPartList` not confirmed; only matters with `useMinQtyAutoGenPOs` on AND a vendor with several vendor parts for one part (ireport DB: property off; local FB_OCT: on, no such case in the run). | On a DB with the property on: a part with two vendor parts of its default vendor and different minimums, compare the stock report (default vendor only) with the port. |  |
| Auto PO | Location groups | One group, or "-1" = every active group of the user added together ("Company Wide"); a comma list can't parse and also falls back to all. | None picked = the same company-wide call (lg null). Several picked = one engine run per group, each group's vendors under the group's heading, with a progress note before each run. | addition | Port BP + Off Site Storage: 2 runs `[1, 2]`, 11 + 22 parts = the stock's `apo_lg1.pdf` (11) and `apo_lg2.pdf` (22). | Filters → pick two location groups → Run; status says "(2 engine runs)". |  |
| Auto PO | QB class with several groups | qbClassID adds every location group of that class to the run's groups (engine). | Passed through on every run, so each per-group run also includes the class's groups (help text says so). | data | `runAutoPo` lines 32–102: `findAllByQBClassId` added to the LG set. | Pick a QB class and two groups; debug console shows each call with the class id. |  |
| Auto PO | Date range — format | dateRange1/2 passed to the engine as dates. | Sends `'YYYY-MM-DD 00:00:00'` / `'YYYY-MM-DD 23:59:59'`: the bridge parses `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss` only and silently drops anything else (no date limit). | data | `Util.parse(s, 4)` = `DATE_SQL_FORMATTER` "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss". Stock with Jan 2026: 16 parts, without dates: 8 → the dates matter; port Jan 2026: the same 16. (`Custom/Inventory/Auto_PO_Inventory_Watchlist.htm` passes `YYYY-MM-DD`, so its dates never reach the engine — its comment "the engine ignores date params" comes from that.) | Custom range 2026-01-01 → 2026-01-31 (ireport data) → 16 parts; debug console shows the two strings. |  |
| Auto PO | Date range — end day | The engine filters `dateScheduledFulfillment BETWEEN start AND end` with midnight dates, so a line scheduled after 00:00 on the end day is left out. | End = 23:59:59: both end days count in full. | fix | `SalesOrderItemRepository.getAllocatedOrderDemand` (between on dateScheduledFulfillment, only when both dates are set). | A line scheduled at 10:00 on the end day: counted by the port, not by the stock report. |  |
| Auto PO | Date range — default | dateRange2 is prompted with no default (the dialog's range picker decides); no saved `reportparameter` rows for report 123. | Default "All dates" (no limit), plus presets "Due within 7/30/60/90 days (and overdue)", This month, Next month, Custom. | data | `SELECT … FROM reportparameter WHERE reportid = 123` → none. | Reset filters → Date range = All dates. |  |
| Auto PO | Running | The report dialog runs the helper once. | The engine is synchronous (blocks the page), so filters wait for **Run** (panel or header), never per keystroke; changed filters glow with "Filters changed: click Run". Runs on opening unless Settings → "Run when the report opens" is off. Overlapping runs are dropped (load token). | addition | Harness: change a filter → no engine call, note shown; Run → one call. autoRun off → reload makes 0 calls. Two Runs back to back → only the second's rows. | Tick a box: the table doesn't change until Run. |  |
| Auto PO | Part column | One cell "PartNum - PartDescription". | Separate Part (links to the Part module) and Description columns. | layout | — | Any run. |  |
| Auto PO | Vendor part numbers | ShowVendorPartNumber prints "VPN (num)" in the part cell (just the number when they match); a vendor part without a number prints " (num)". | A Vendor Part # column (when the run had the option on); Part keeps the Fishbowl number. Blank when there is none or it equals the part number. | layout | Stock: "423478 (FOOD124)", " (CONS050)"; port: Part FOOD124 / Vendor Part # 423478; CONS050 / blank. | Show vendor part numbers + Only default → Run. |  |
| Auto PO | UOMs | The part's UOM printed after every figure, and the vendor UOM after Needed. | One UOM column (the part's) and a Vendor UOM column (Needed's unit). | layout | — | Any run. |  |

| Auto PO | No-vendor heading | The group expression prints "No Vendor" for a null vendor, but the engine returns "" so the heading prints blank. | "No Vendor" (italic on screen). | fix | `apo_default_only_vpn.pdf`, `apo_lg1.pdf`: blank group heading over CONS050 / I500CK06 / I5LTCO01. | Same runs. |  |
| Auto PO | Group order | Rows in the engine's list order; the engine replaces a part's row in place when a later vendor needs more (vendor minimums), which can put another vendor's row inside the first vendor's group and split a vendor into two headings. | Sorted by vendor name (No Vendor first, as the empty name sorts), vendor part number when shown, part number: each vendor is one heading. | fix | `LogicInventory.addOrReplaceAutoPoData` (`List.set` at the old index). Not seen on either DB. | A part with two vendors where only the later-named one has a minimum above the need (useMinQtyAutoGenPOs on). |  |

## Sales Order and Ship (paperwork)

The business documents: Sales Order Report and Sales Invoice (one page, the
Invoice setting), Packing Slip, Shipping Invoice. Since 2026-10-16 they run on
the REPORT chrome with an App view and a PDF view; the PDF view is the
proof-of-concept document unchanged (same sheets on every test case).

| Report | Area | Stock report does | Port does | Type | Evidence | How to verify | Reviewed |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sales Order Report, Sales Invoice, Packing Slip, Shipping Invoice | Screen | The printed pages only | App view (the record on screen: card, addresses, fields, lines with table tools, totals, cartons / payments / memos) and PDF view (the printed document), toggled in the header; Excel saves the lines | addition | 2026-10-16: printed sheets identical to the earlier paper-only pages on every case (tools/doc-tests, 62 tests; A/B sheet text) | Open each document: App view; toggle to PDF |  |
| Sales Order Report, Sales Invoice, Packing Slip, Shipping Invoice | Options | The stock report's saved options | The same, as the starting point; each user's choices are remembered (Settings › Remember); an admin's company defaults replace the earlier "Save for everyone" (still read until replaced) | addition | not stated | Change an option, reopen: it stays; Settings › Admin |  |

## Open questions

1. **Location-group scope.** Reorder Report limits location groups to the
   user's own "as the Consumption port", and On Hand By Date lists the same
   limit under its Differences. Most other ports say it matches the iReport
   default. Check what the stock dialog passes, then make the ports agree.
